### □ I. IMPORTANT NOTICE

The Board of Directors and the Directors of Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd. (hereinafter referred to as "the Company") undertake that the information in the Report does not contain any misrepresentation, misleading statements or materials omissions and jointly and individually accept full responsibility for the truthfulness, accuracy and completeness of the contents.

The main body and the summary of the Annual Report of 2009 were approved at the 45 $ ^{th} $ session of the 3 $ ^{rd} $ Board of Directors.

The financial report of 2009 has been audited by Shulun Pan Certified Public Accountants Co., Ltd. in line with the Domestic Accounting Standard for Business Enterprise. Shulun Pan CPAs has prepared a standard auditors' report with unqualified opinion.

Chairman Xia Ruizhou, President Xing Zengfu, Vice President in charge of finance Jin Jiankang, and General Manager of Planning & Financial Department Zhang Demin hereby confirm that the annual financial report is true and complete.

(3) The change in “retained profit” was due to the increase of net profit for the reporting period, which increased retained profit amounting to RMB 444,655,000. The Company appropriated surplus reserve of RMB 332,326,000 yuan, provision for general risk of RMB 27,841,000 yuan and dividends payable for 2009 amounting to RMB 102,318,000, which decreased retained profit amounting to RMB 17,830,000.

## III. Loans Classified to Five Classes at the end of the reporting period

## V. Non-performing Loans at the end of the reporting period and the actions the Company should take

During the reporting period, to be confronted with a lot of unstabilizing factors brought by macro-economy and the sluggish international market, the Company implemented the effective collection plan against the non-performing loans, resulting in reduction in both non-performing loans ratio and balance. As at the end of the reporting period, the balance of non-performing loan according to the five-category classification standard was RMB 219,180,000 yuan, a decrease of RMB 78,720,000 yuan compared with that at the beginning of the year. The non-performing loan ratio was 0.89%, down by 0.56% than that at the beginning of the year. During the reporting period, the Company adopted a series of effective measures suppressing the increase of non-performing loan: the first was actively responding to our country macro-economic control, tightening the standards of loan and credit approval, carrying out predictive withdrawal system and optimizing the credit assets structure. The second was reinforcing and regulating business operation management and strictly carrying out the loan examination system (i.e. pre-loan-issuance investigation, loan issuance examination and post-loan-issuance examination). The third was making more efforts to collect and dispose non-performing loans and specifying the key points of loan collection; reinforcing the collection of new overdue loans and effectively collecting non-performing loans. The fourth was establishing a multi-level organization structure, specifying the responsibilities of each level and collecting and disposing non-performing loans in a centralized manner.

(viii) Risk management over credit line for group customers

During the reporting period, the Company revised the Management Measures on Credit Line for Group Customers, persisting in the unified, moderate and precaution principles: the first was managing and controlling the risk of credit line for group customers and in centralized manner. In principle, branches (sub-branches) would manage the credit line to all related companies of the group customers. The second was setting an overall credit line to the group customers, which was no more than 15% of the net capital of the Company. The third was closely examining the customer's condition after the loan was issued and learning the related information and analyzing the business situation of the group customer through various channels to prevent and eliminate the credit risk of group customers.

Notes: (1) Bank acceptance bill is a credit loan business in which the payee or payer (or cashing applicant) issues a commercial acceptance bill and the cashing applicant applies to the Bank and the Bank agrees to cash the commercial acceptance bill after examination.

(2) L/G is a credit loan business in which the Bank is requested by the applicant or authorized person to issue a letter of guarantee promising to the beneficiary that when the applicant fails to fulfill the liabilities and undertakings set in the contract, the Bank will cover the debt or bear the liabilities as ruled in the letter of guarantee.

(3) L/C is a credit loan in which the Bank is requested and instructed by the applicant to issue a written guarantee letter of certain amount to the beneficiary which guarantees to pay the amount in a given period at a given place upon given documents.

(4) Loan undertakings refer to that ager negotiation which the customer, the Bank issues a letter of undertaking to the customer undertaking to issue a certain amount of loan to the customer in certain period.

## XVI. Risk and Countermeasures of the Company

The Company faced credit risk, liquidity risk, market risk and operating risk. Recognizing these risks fully, the Company had engaged in improving risk management ability. The Company established an effective risk management system and prudent risk management culture through establishing a Risk Control Committee and formulating relevant working rules. During the reporting period, the Company revised the Guidelines for Overall Risk Management of Bank of Wenzhou, kept optimizing the risk management organizational structure and perfecting the policies and process for risk management. By using advanced risk measurement and management tools, the Company tried to realize sustainable growth of business while keeping high assets quality. In the reporting period, no risk that imposed materials influences to the business operation of the Company occurred.

#### (i) Credit risk management

Credit risk refers to the possibility of loss and uncertainty of income of the bank due to the customer's breach of agreement or deterioration of credit in the Company's credit, loan and investment business. The credit risk of the Bank mainly exists in the on-balance-sheet and off-balance-sheet business including loan, inter-bank lending, bond investment, bill acceptance, L/C and letter of indemnity of the bank.

During the reporting period, the Company followed the principle of carrying out the macroeconomic regulation policy, targeted to improving the risk management ability and focused on optimizing asset structure and improving asset quality to promote risk management and ensure all businesses of the Company to develop in a balanced, orderly and healthy way. According to the requirement of the macroeconomic regulation policy, the Company tightened credit approval standards. The Company balanced loan issue to avoid negative influence of the adjustment of industrial policy and the periodical industrial fluctuation on the loan and improve the efficiency of the loan capital. By reinforcing policy guidance to credit policy and controlling over credit approval, the Company actively adjusted loan issuing direction and optimized credit structure. During the credit approving process, the Company provided green channel for loans to industrial sectors supported by the government, restricted the credit approving object and total credit amount for loans to industrial sectors regulate by the government, and formulated cutting-down and withdrawing approaches for loans to industrial sectors restricted by the government so as to reinforce structure adjustment and control from the source. By reinforcing active countermeasures such as early risk warning, reinforcing post-loan examination and making more efforts in collecting and disposing existing and new non-performing loans, the Company controlled and relieved credit risk.

#### (ii) Liquidity risk management

Liquidity risk refers to the risk due to unable to cash the assets at a reasonable price in order to provide fund for due debts. The liquidity risks of the Company are mainly resulted from activities providing funds such as loans, transactions and investment as well as management over current assets.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_1086_190_1150_357.jpg" alt="Image" width="5%" /></div>


During the reporting period, by internal capital transferring system, the Company manages liquidity of the bank in a centralized manner. In the aspect of assets, the Company keeps a property percentage of short-term assets such as bill discount asset and highly liquid inter-bank lending, repurchase agreement and investment in central bank bills to ensure adequate liquidity. In the aspect of liabilities, the Company raises money from various channels including the public market of the central bank, inter-bank monetary market and bill market of the financial industry to ensure financing channels unblocked and daily liquidity demand satisfied. The Company sets various percentage standards (including but not limited to deposit/loan ratio, standby deposit ratio and setting up instructive ratio goal for every branches and sub-branches) and limit to transaction amount according to the overall asset and liability status in order to monitor and manage liquidity risk. The Company has also established the early warning system for liquidity risk and the liquidity management system to monitor the fund balance of each branch or sub-branch every day in order to keep safe liquidity.

During the reporting period, the Company reinforced asset and liability management in time under the monetary policy and regulatory requirements of the regulatory authority and according to the growth of the deposit and loan of the Company to control the liquidity effectively. In general, the liquidity of the Company kept stable. At the end of the reporting period, the positive liquidity gap of the Company was RMB 1.506 billion yuan. The liquidity gap for real-time repayment was RMB -13.282 billion yuan, which was because the Company had a high amount of deposit that would be mature in three months and a high amount deposit on call, balance being RMB 23.11 billion yuan, accounting for 68.55% of the deposit of customers. Therefore, liquidity risk of real-time repayment was actually low.

#### (iii) Market risk management

Market risk refers to the risk of loss on the non-balance-sheet and off-balance-sheet transactions of the bank due to negative changes in market price (interest rate, foreign exchange rate, stock price and commodity price). The market risk of the Company mainly includes interest rate and foreign exchange risk.

During the reporting period, the market risk measurement methods of the Company include but not limited to VAR, gap analysis, mark to market, duration analysis, sensitivity analysis, pressure test and post hoc test. The Company has established regular market risk analysis reporting system and provisional major event reporting system and Market Risk Management Department informs the management team and Broad of Directors about the latest market risk status in time.

### 1. Interest rate risk

Interest rate risk refers to the risk of negative influence on the financial status of the Company due to the fluctuation of the market interest rate. The interest risk of the Company is mainly caused by the incompliance of the terms of asset and liability transactions and the influence on the equity of the Company from the fluctuation of market interest rate. According to the changes in the market interest rate and reality of the Company, the Company adjusts the duration of assets and liabilities in time, scientifically and properly. The Company is targeted to keep the steady growth of net interest income in short term and to increase the equity of the Company steadily in long term to reduce interest rate risk.

### 2. Foreign exchange rate risk

Foreign exchange rate refers to the risk of negative influence on the financial status due to the fluctuation of the market foreign exchange rate. The foreign exchange risk of the Company is mainly derived from foreign currency open position. In view of the increasing international financial market unrest, the severe fluctuation of the exchange rate of main currencies and the increasingly big two-way fluctuation of CNY, the Company followed rational, long-term and prudential principles, carried out balanced management over assets and liabilities, actively adjusted the structure assets and liabilities in foreign currencies and optimized internal risk control system to lower the risk of open position and the risk of foreign exchange rate.

#### (iv) Operating risk management

Operating risk refers to the risk of loss caused by imperfect or problematic internal process, staff and IT system and external affairs. By such measures as setting up operating risk management system, reinforcing internal control, performing operating risk indicator monitor, launching operating risk investigation, improving staff's awareness and ability to guard against risk, and strictly carrying out accountability system, the Company standardized operating risk management step by step and strengthened operating risk prevention.

During the reporting period, the Company launched such rules and regulations as Indicator System for Operating Risk Monitor of Bank of Wenzhou in order to intensify operating risk monitoring and management, and perfect operating risk management system. According to the arrangement of the Department for CBRC, the Company, with special treatment of case as breakthrough point, actively organized to launch a campaign of "Hundred-Day Major Investigation" against case risk, mainly focusing on such six aspects as wholesale deposits business, loan transaction, billing services management, credit card service, employee behavior management and performance appraisal measure management. Through examination, some problems and shortcomings were discovered in the Company's implementation level of system and certain business fields and links, therefore, the Company strengthened accountability enforcement against any behaviors violating rules, and proposed the specific proposals and measures in order to improve management; carried out 10 business departments to regularly monitor 27 operating risk monitoring indicators, including such risk monitoring as credit business, services over the counter, international business, safety and security, computer information, law and compliance. Meanwhile, the Company estimated objectively the operating risk management of all business lines and adopted effective improvement measures to enhance operating risk management level.

## XVII. Notes to Completeness, Reasonableness and Validity of Internal Control

Following the Company Law of the People's Republic of China, Law of the People's Republic of China on Commercial Banks, Guidelines for Internal Control of Commercial Banks and Guidance on Corporate Governance of Joint Stock Commercial Banks and integrating the advanced management modes of domestic and overseas commercial banks, the Company optimized the internal organization structure and has established a corporate governance frame consisting of Shareholders' General Meeting, Board of Directors, Supervisory Board and the top management and realized the operating system that ownership, operating right and supervising right are separated and inter-restrictive. The Company introduced in and passed ISO9000 quality management system certification, established and perfected controlling system for authorization and approval, power and responsibility separation, credit and loan risk control, accounting system control, information system control and assets in kind control, which are all carried out effectively. Oriented by overall risk management, the Company reinforced credit risk, liquidity risk, market risk and operating risk management. The Company assessed the internal control status of the headquarters and branches annually, monitored and assessed the business activities constantly and disclosed defects in internal control and gave suggestions on improvement. The Company recognized, measured and recorded valid economic transaction pursuant to rules and regulations. The advanced computer software and internal network system provided technology support for reflecting various kinds of business information timely and correctly. The financial report of 2009 properly disclosed the financial status, operating results and cash flow of the year. Smooth communications between the management and the internal departments and external department were ensured, including communications with related interested parties, regulatory department and CPAs. The chief accountant designation system of the Company could ensure branch institutions recording various transactions timely, correctly and completely.

#### (ii) Shares issued over the past three years as at the end of the reporting period

1. During the reporting period, the Company implemented plan of allotment of shares for year 2009 at the rate of 3 for 10 to the original shareholders at the price of RMB 3 yuan per share, totaling 301,226,691 shares, with a RMB 903,680,073 yuan share placement. #### (v) Information of shareholders holding 5% or above shares of the Company

1. China Huarong Asset Management Corporation

Legal representative: Lai Xiaomin;

Date of incorporation: 1999;

Main business: acquiring and operating the non-performing assets split from ICBC; debt recovery, asset exchange, assignment and sales; debt restructuring and corporation restructuring; creditor's right transferring to equity and phase shareholding, asset securitization; IPO recommendation and bond, share underwriting within the asset management scope; direct investment; bond issuance; commercial loans; borrowing from financial institutions and applying for re-borrowing from the People's Bank of China; investment, financial and legal consultation and advice; asset and project evaluation; business audit and bankruptcy liquidation; and other business approved by the financial regulatory authority.

Registered capital: RMB 10 billion yuan;

Equity structure: solely state owned.

2. Changtai Holding Group Co., Ltd.

Legal representative: Chen Shengquan

Date of incorporation: 1995

Main business: power engineering and construction, electricity equipment maintenance, power development and operation, electric energy test, power engineering design and technical consultation service, production and installation of power products and auxiliary equipment, real estate development and operation, property management, information consultation service, sales of metal materials, construction materials and chemical materials and products (excluding hazardous chemicals), sales of coal, lease service.

Registered capital: RMB 254 million yuan

Equity structure: Ji Lingwu invests RMB 7.384 million yuan, accounting for 2.91%; Chen Shengquan invests RMB 38.168 million yuan, accounting for 15.03%; Zhang Yiyun invests RMB 39.104 million yuan, accounting for 15.40%; Jiang Yong invests RMB 46.80 million yuan, accounting for 18.43%; Chen Rongzhu invests RMB 17.42 million yuan, accounting for 6.86%; Xu Xiangdong invests RMB 11.232 million yuan, accounting for 4.42%; Dai Xiaolong invests RMB 22.802 million yuan, accounting for 8.98%; and Zhou Hanrong invests RMB 71.09 million yuan, accounting for 27.99%.

3. Zhe Jiang Dongri Company Limited

Legal representative: Zheng Nianhong

Date of incorporation: 1997

Main business: industrial investment; market lease and operation; development of computer networks, production and sales of lamps and lamp parts, and property management.

Registered capital: RMB 147.50 million yuan

Equity structure: state-owned shareholders hold 78,475,000 shares, accounting for 53.20%; tradable shareholders hold 69,025,000 shares, accounting for 46.80%.

4. Wenzhou Jiahong Real Estate Development Co., Ltd.

Legal representative: Zhou Dingwen

Date of incorporation: 2001

Main business: development and operation of real estate; upholstery services

Registered capital: RMB 100 million

Equity structure: Shanghai Huahong Investment (Group) Co., Ltd.

5. Hong Qing Ting Group Co., Ltd.

Legal representative: Qian Jinbo

Date of incorporation: 1996

Main business and products: leather products; apparels, auxiliary materials for shoes, knitting goods, daily commodities; hardware and electric appliances, raw materials and auxiliary materials, mechanical equipment, instruments and meters, parts and components, and processing with customer provided materials.

Registered capital: RMB 185 million yuan

Equity structure: Qian Jinbo invests RMB 90.65 million yuan, accounting for 49%; Jin Yinkuan invests RMB 27.75 million yuan, accounting for 15%; Zheng Chunming invests RMB 20.35 million yuan, accounting for 11%; Chen Minghai invests RMB 18.5 million yuan, accounting for 10%; Xia Saixia invests RMB 14.8 million yuan, accounting for 8%; and Huang Rongxin invests RMB 12.95 million yuan, accounting for 7%.

## II. Work experiences and positions of current directors, supervisors and senior managerial staff

#### (i) Directors

Mr. Xia Ruizhou, was born in November 1946, undergraduate degree and senior economist, used to be Deputy Secretary of CPC Luofu District Committee and Head of Luofu District, Yongjia County, Wenzhou, Committee Member of CPC Yongjia County Committee, Secretary of the CPC Committee of Qiaotou Town; Committee Member of CPC Yongjia County Committee, Secretary of the CPC Committee of Oubei Town; Deputy Secretary of CPC Wencheng County Committee and County Mayor of Wencheng County; Deputy Director of the Construction Office of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank, Currently, he is Chairman of Bank of Wenzhou.

Mr. Xing Zengfu, was born in October 1963, master degree and senior accountant, used to be Vice President of ICBC Linhai Sub-Branch; President of ICBC Jiaojiang Sub-Branch; Director of the Business Department and Peony Card Department of ICBC Taizhou Branch; Chairman and General Manager of Sino-Foreign Joint Venture Zhejiang Guangsha Property Company, Vice President of ICBC Wenzhou Branch. Currently, he is President and Vice Chairman of Bank of Wenzhou.

Mr. Huang Chenyuan, was born in April 1954, junior college degree, senior economist, used to be Chief of staff of the navy, Director of the Government Bond Office of Wenzhou Municipal Finance Bureau, Deputy Director of the Construction Office of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank, Assistant to President of Wenzhou City Commercial Bank. Currently, he is Director and Vice President of Bank of Wenzhou and concurrently Secretary of the Board of Bank of Wenzhou.

Mr. Chen Chaohui, was born in May 1963, Ph.D and professor, used to be a research of Guangzhou Economic Research Institute, Lecturer of EU Economics, Jean Monnet and Researcher of Financial Market Research Center, London School of Economics and Political Science, Vice President of JPMorgan, Director of the First Light Academy, U.S., CEO of Global Education Management Co., Ltd. ## VII. Staff

As of the end of the reporting period, the Company has 1666 employees on the payroll, including 395 managerial staff, 1271 frontline staff and 46 administrative staff. In the employees in service, 48.92% of them have undergraduate degree or above; 43.94% have junior college degree, and 7.14% have below junior college degree.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_1086_191_1150_358.jpg" alt="Image" width="5%" /></div>


# VII. Corporate Governance Structure

## I. Corporate Governance

The Company strictly observed the provisions of Company Law of the People's Republic of China and the Law of Commercial Banks of the People's Republic of China, and carried out the requirements of the regulatory authorities on corporate governance. Taking account of the actual condition of the Company, the Company promoted the corporate governance system construction structured based on the Board, the Supervisory Board and the management, and optimized the corporate governance structure of which decisions made by the Board, full operation made by operation management team and supervised by the Supervisory Board. The regulatory level of corporate governance was improved effectively.

During the reporting period, the Company amended the Responsible Appraisal Method for Directors, and formulated the Information Report System of Top Management, which achieved normalization and institutionalization of information reporting made by senior management to the Board and further perfected the basic rules for corporate governance, providing safeguard for the normative operation of the corporate governance of the Company.

##### (i) Shareholders and shareholders' general meeting

As at the end of the reporting period, the Company has 1909 shareholders in total, including 1 state shareholder, 7 state-owned corporate shareholders, 97 other corporate shareholders and 1804 natural-person shareholders.

During the reporting period, the Company held the annual shareholders' general meeting 2008 and the 1 $ ^{st} $ extraordinary shareholders' general meeting 2009 strictly in accordance with the requirements of the Articles of Association of the Company. Both two meetings hired Beijing Tianyin Law Firm to be present at the meetings for witness and produce legal opinion. Such sixteen key issues as the Working Report of the Board, the Report on Business Plan Fulfillment in 2008 and Business Plan for 2009 and the Report on Implementing Capital Increase was examined and approved at the meetings.

#### (ii) Directors and the Board of Directors

As at the end of the reporting period, the Board of Directors had 18 members, including 1 Chairman, 1 Vice Chairman, 6 independent directors, 9 shareholder directors, and 1 management director and concurrently secretary of the Board. In the reporting period, the Board held 12 meetings in total, discussed 73 proposals and adopted 54 resolutions. All directors seriously performed their duties and effectively played out their decision-making function to maintain overall interests of all shareholders and the Company.

During the reporting period, the Board adjusted Strategic Development Planning of the Company at the right time, accelerated the pace of cross-region operation, establishing Hangzhou Branch, perfected layout within our province, preparing to construct Shanghai Branch and overfulfiling trans-provincial development, so as to further carry forward the Company's development of operation strategy with keeping a foothold in Wenzhou, making overall arrangement in Zhejiang and promoting development in Yangtze River Delta. The Board implemented the Allotment of Shares for 2009 and the Proposal on Private Placement of Additional Shares and pushed on issuance of hybrid capital bond, so as to actively widen the source of capital replenishment and ensure sustainable and solid development in the Bank's businesses. The Board reinforced risk control mechanism, establishing credit risk executive officer mechanism, revising the Guidelines on Overall Risk Management, regularly analyzing and appraising

the Company's risk situation, strengthening the control of related parties transaction, and intensifying information management of the Company, so as to steadily advance overall risk management. The Board also optimized internal control mechanism and intensified management of internal control standard, amending Detailed Implementing Rules for Related Transaction Management, perfecting ISO9000 QMS, launching special audit, pushing on prevention of case, implementing internal control assessment, optimizing economic capital appraisal, so as to improve capacity of internal control management in real earnest. The Board also deepened corporate governance, perfected business operation system, enhanced duty performance capacity of directors and improved quality of information disclosure, formulating the Information Report System of Senior Management, strengthening the transmission mechanism for information, and reinforcing authorization management, so as to promote the Company's coordinated development among benefit, quality and scale by the effective corporate governance.

#### (iii) Supervisors and Supervisory Board

During the reporting period, the Supervisory Board has 9 members, including 1 chief supervisor, 2 external supervisors, 3 shareholder supervisors and 3 staff-representative supervisors. By holding regular meetings, reviewing various documents reported by the Company, hearing the work report and special reports of the management, participating in internal control inspection and assessment, the Supervisory Board launched examination and supervision to the Company's operation status, financial activities and internal control and risk management. In the reporting period, the Supervisory Board appointed intermediary agency to audit the financial and operation status, internal control and risk management for year 2008 and carried out all tasks of tripartite dialogue among the regulatory department, the intermediary agency and the Company. The Supervisory Board supervised the credit quality, main regulatory indicators and risk control situation and expressed supervision opinion regularly; participated in campaigns such as the internal post-loan management examination, special treatment of case and comprehensive treatment of social security. Meanwhile, the Supervisory Board also made feedback and communication and gave opinions and advices to the management for the problems found in supervision and audit.

During the reporting period, the members of the Supervisory Board attended 2 shareholders' general meetings, 5 Board meetings as a non-voting delegate and 18 president administration meetings as a non-voting delegate to supervise the legality and conformity of the shareholders' general meeting, the Board and the top management, the voting procedures and the conformity and duty performance of the directors and the top management members. During the reporting period, the Supervisory Board also made economic responsibility audit of one president and two vice presidents during their office term and assessed the duty performance of the directors, supervisors and the top management members.

The Audit Committee of the Supervisory Board made communication with Shulun Pan CPAs Co., Ltd. concerning the audit of 2008 and audited and analyzed the financial and operation management status, internal control and risk management for year 2008.

##### (iv) Senior management

During the reporting period, the Company's president room consists of 4 members, including one president and 3 vice presidents, who implement effective management and overall control to the Company's operation. The Company organized to convene 1 annual working meeting of president and 3 quarterly meetings of branch president to discuss and analyze economic and financial situation, review the work at different stages and analyze problems and shortcoming existed at work, and set forth work objective for the period ahead and main measures. The top management held 7 specialized meetings to study and make measures on subjects such as implementation of regulatory requirements, capital and share increase, overcoming risk loan of YUTIAN Group, job interview for senior managerial staff. The top management held 3 bank affairs meetings, deliberating on the

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_1086_190_1151_357.jpg" alt="Image" width="5%" /></div>


implementation of the spirit of the annual working conference of president and questions and proposals put forward by all branches and sub-branches during the investigation, studying the annual regulatory opinion and implementation of "tripartite dialogue" rectification, and transmitting the spirit of the case prevention and security working meeting of Zhejiang provincial banking. The top management also held 18 president administration meetings to study and discuss 160 proposals including annual assessment and remuneration management program for branch offices and head office, banking offices adjustment for 2009, capital replenishment program for 2009, competition methods for marketing of bank businesses, framework and operation of bill center, carrying out implementation plans on the Guidelines on IT Risk Management of Commercial Bank, proposal on directive breeding of professional through cooperation between the bank and the universities, trilateral cooperation project for SME loans, measures for assessment and rewarding the prime bank in credit quality, fund distribution service, provisional measures for the administration of credit risk executive officer mechanism, and make science decisions. In 2009, the management fulfilled its duties and obligations according to Company Law of the People's Republic of China and the Articles of Association, carried out various resolutions of the Shareholders' General Meeting and the Board of Directors, ensured effective execution of laws, rules and internal regulations by unceasingly improving executive force of internal control, so as to promote the sustained and sound development of the Company's business. The overall strength and social influence of the Company kept improving.

#### (v) Information disclosure and transparency

According to relevant requirements of information disclosure, the Company improved the timeliness, accuracy and completeness of information disclosure and ensured all shareholders accessing the information equally. During the reporting period, the Company finished the preparation of the Annual Report 2008 on time in line with the Rules for Management of Information Disclosure and published the Summary of the Annual Report 2008 in Financial Times. The Company also provided the Report in the Office of the Board and branches for investors and stakeholder for reference. Meanwhile, the Company handled mails, calls, visits and questions of shareholders seriously to safeguard the legal rights and interests of vast shareholders in an effective way.

## II. Duty Performance of Independent Directors and External Supervisors

The Company has 6 independent directors on the Board and 2 external supervisors who take office of chairman on all special committees. In the reporting period, the independent directors and external supervisors respectively attended the meetings of the Board and the Supervisors Board in a positive attitude, and seriously organized to call the meetings of all special committees under the Board or the Supervisory Board. They expressed their independent opinions actively and played effective roles as independent directors and external supervisors.

## III. Independent Operation of the Company

The Company has no any controlling shareholder and actual controller. The Company keeps full independent in business, personnel, assets, organization and finance under the supervision of the People's Bank of China and China Banking Regulatory Commission. The Company is an independent corporation which runs business independently and is entitled or assumes sole responsibility for its own profits and losses. The Company has independent and complete business and independent operation ability.

The highest power body of the Company is the Shareholders' General Meeting, which carries out management and supervision through the Board of Directors and the Supervisory Board. The President is appointed by the Board of Directors and takes full charge of the daily business management of the Company. The Company adopts first-level corporate system. All branches are non-independent accounting units. Their business activities are operated under the authorization of the headquarters and they report to the headquarters.

## IV. Assessment and Incentive Mechanism for Senior Managerial Staff

During the reporting period, the assessment indicators for the senior managerial staff included whether he fulfilled the decisions of the Board and the fulfillment of the business goals given by the Board and whether he actively maintained the interests of shareholders and retained and increased the value of the assets of the Company. With 15 indicators in four aspects, i.e., business development, risk control, business performance and business operation conformity, the assessment result, rewarding and penalty were closely related to the compensation. Meanwhile, the Company's top management accepted the management of the Board and the supervision of the Supervisory Board and reported to the Board and the Supervisory Board on the financial status and operation results of the Company to ensure directors and supervisors to be informed about the daily business operation and management of the Company constantly.

The incentive and restraining mechanism of the Company was embodied in the assessment and compensation system of the senior managerial staff. According to the Articles of Association, the compensation policy and assessment system are determined by the Board. The fulfillment of the business goal of the management, the rewarding plan for over-fulfilling profit and the management rules on the performance assessment of the top management were reported to and approved by the Nomination and Remuneration Committee under the Board and finally determined by the Board.

# ☐ VIII. Shareholders' General Meeting

## I. Annual Shareholders' General Meeting

The Annual Shareholders' General Meeting 2008 was held at Onehome Howard Johnson Hotel Wenzhou on 29 April 2009. Eighty shareholders and authorized representative of the shareholders were present at the meeting, holding 842,303,029 shares with valid voting right, accounting for 82.32% of the total shares of the Company. The following 10 proposals were reviewed and approved at this meeting, i.e., Working Report of the Board, the Report on Business Plan Fulfillment in 2008 and Business Plan for 2009, Financial Final Report for 2008 and Financial Budget Report for 2009, Final Report on Profit Distribution for 2007, Report on Profit Distribution Preplan for 2008, Working Report of the Supervisory Board, Report on Adjusting Development Plan for 2008-2010 of Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd., Proposal on Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd. Intending to Issue Hybrid Capital Bond and Offer Plan, Proposal on Authorizing the Board and the Management to Handle Specific Matters on Issuing Hybrid Capital Bond by Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd. and Proposal on Utilization of Raised Capital through Issuing Hybrid Capital Bond by Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd. and Feasibility Analysis. The following 3 reports were heard at this meeting, i.e., the Report on Carrying out Regulatory Opinion and Rectification of on-the-spot Inspection for 2008, Report on the Implementation of Rules of Management of Related Parties Transactions and Related Parties Transaction for 2008, and Duty Performance of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Managerial Staff for 2008.

## II. Extraordinary Shareholders' General Meeting

The 1 $ ^{st} $ extraordinary shareholders" general meeting was held at Onehome Howard Johnson Hotel Wenzhou on 11 Nov. 2009. Ninety-one shareholders and authorized representative of the shareholders were present at the meeting, holding 968,006,928 shares with valid voting right, accounting for 94.61% of the total shares of the Company. The proposals such as the Proposal on Implementation of Allotment of Shares for 2009 and the Proposal on Private Placement of Additional Shares were reviewed and approved at this meeting, and the Report on Implementing Capital Increase was heard at this meeting.

## II. Discussion and Analysis of the Management

##### (i) Scope of main business

Approved by the China Banking Regulatory Commission, the Company is mainly engaged in: receiving public deposits; issuing short-term, mid-term and long-term loans; handling domestic and overseas settlement; handling bill acceptance and discount; issuing financial bonds; issuing, cashing and selling government bonds as an agent; buying and selling government bonds and financial bonds; operating inter-bank borrowings and lendings; operating bankcard business; providing L/C service and guarantee; collecting and paying funds and selling insurances as an agent; providing safe box service; purchasing and selling or acting as an agent to purchase and sell foreign currencies; providing guarantee letters; and other businesses approved by the China Banking Regulatory Commission.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_1086_191_1149_358.jpg" alt="Image" width="5%" /></div>


##### (ii) Overall business development

In 2009, the Company carried out an operation and management transformation, accelerated trans-regional development, kept forging ahead and effectively boosted sound development in all businesses. As a result, the set annual business objectives were excellently achieved. Meanwhile, phasic strategic objectives were also achieved. For instance, the Hangzhou branch was successfully opened up, the Shanghai branch was approved to set up, and the share placement and the additional share issuance were implemented smoothly. Both the scale and quality of the Company increased steadily with improving corporate governance and a remarkable increase in the comprehensive strength. Relevant details are as follows:

1. The overall business scale grew steadily. Total assets as at the year-end stood at RMB 41.017 billion, up by RMB 6.876 billion from that at the year-begin, representing a growth of 20.14%, in which balance of various loans granted stood at RMB 24.593 billion (including discounts), up by RMB 4.114 billion from that at the year-begin, representing a growth of 20.09%. Total liabilities stood at RMB 38.405 billion, up by RMB 6.064 billion from that at the year-begin, representing a growth of 18.75%, in which balance of various deposits stood at RMB 33.711 billion, up by RMB 6.053 billion from that at the year-begin, representing a growth of 21.89%. Income from intermediary business stood at RMB 53,135,300, down by RMB 1,361,000 as compared with that of last year, representing a decrease of 2.50%.

2. Profitability maintained stable. For the year 2009, the Company achieved an operating income of RMB 1.198 billion, down by RMB 43 million, or 3.45%, as compared with that of last year; a total profit of RMB 551 million, down by RMB 42 million, or 7.08%, as compared with that of last year; a net profit after tax of RMB 445 million, down by RMB 21 million, or 4.61%, as compared with that of last year; and owners' equity of RMB 2.611 billion, up by RMB 811 million, or 45.05%, as compared with last year. Affected by the expanding bank credit, interest income for the year 2009 decreased due to lower loan interest rates and interest expenses increased due to more term deposits. As such, profit for the year 2009 registered a slight decrease but maintained at a stable level.

3. Operating cost increased to some degree. During the reporting period, new branches such as the Hangzhou branch, the Jiangshan sub-branch of the Quzhou branch and the Beibaixiang sub-branch were set up with considerable increase in labor cost and rental expenses. At the period-end, the income-expense rate stood at 28.79%, representing a year-on-year growth of 8.75%.

4. The capital sufficiency rate was effectively increased. At the period-end, the capital sufficiency rate stood at 11.60%, up by 1% from that at the year-begin; and the core capital sufficiency rate stood at 9.06%, up by 1.63% from that at the year-begin.

5. Non-performing loans decreased in two aspects. Upon efforts in loan collection, both the non-performing loan rate and balance were decreased. At the year-end, balance of five-category non-performing loans stood at RMB 219.18 million, down by RMB 78.72 million from that at the year-begin, with the non-performing loan rate standing at 0.89%, down by 0.56% as compared with that at the year-begin. The NPL provision coverage rate stood at 151%, up by 40.87% from that at the year-begin. And the loan loss provision sufficiency rate stood at 150.79%, up by 7.59% as compared with that at the year-begin.

6. The branch layout was further optimized and cross-regional development was fruitful. During the reporting period, the Company made more efforts in setting up new branches and adjusting existing branches. The Beibaixiang sub-branch was newly set up. The Zhanqian sub-branch, the Jiefang Road sub-branch and the Qinye sub-branch were relocated. Meanwhile, the Hangzhou branch and the Quzhou Jiangshan sub-branch successfully opened up for business. And it was approved by CBRC to set up the Shanghai branch. As such, significant breakthroughs were made in the cross-regional operation.

7. Social influence kept increasing. In the reporting period, the cross-regional development strategy for advancing into the Yangtze River Delta was steadily carried forward. The capital increase for the reporting period moved on smoothly with remarkably improved capital strength and capability to withstand risks. Various regulatory indicators of the Company met relevant requirements and the Company was rated the second grade in the regulatory rating. And the Company was granted in the reporting period a series of honorable titles of “Most Influential Small and Medium Banks in China”, “Best Small and Medium Financial Service Banks in China”, “Substantial Tax Payers in Wenzhou (No.4), etc.. As such, the social influence of the Company kept increasing.

##### (iii) Operation of main businesses

1. The corporate banking business: As at the period-end, balance of ordinary corporate deposits stood at RMB 21.6 billion, accounting for 64.07% of the total deposits of customers, representing a growth of RMB 4.463 billion, or 26.04%, as compared with that at the period-begin. And balance of loans to corporations stood at RMB 12.288 billion, accounting for 49.97% of total loans to customers, representing a growth of RMB 2.103 billion, or 20.65%, as compared with that at the period-begin.

2. The personal banking business: As at the period-end, balance of personal deposits stood at RMB 12.11 billion, accounting for 35.92% of total deposits of customers, representing a growth of RMB 1.589 billion, or 15.10%, as compared with that at the year-begin. And balance of loans to individuals stood at RMB 12.305 billion, accounting for 50.03% of total loans to customers, representing a growth of RMB 2.011 billion, or 19.54%, as compared with that at the period-begin.

3. The intermediary business: For the reporting period, the Company achieved an income of RMB 53.14 million from the intermediary business, down by RMB 1.36 million, or 2.50%, from that in the previous year. A total of 847,700 Jinlu debit cards were issued accumulatively, 69,000 more than those at the year-begin, and a total of 36,200 credit cards (active cards) were issued accumulatively, 8,200 more than those at the year-begin. As such, the bankcard business generated an income of RMB 29.02 million (excluding credit card interest income), representing a year-on-year growth of RMB 9.02 million, or 45.10%.

## V. Investments Made by the Company

##### (i) Outward Investments

##### (ii) Use of Raised Funds in Reporting Period

In the reporting period, the Company raised funds of RMB 1,503,680,073 by carrying out share placement and targeted additional share issuance. To be specific, shareholder qualifications of organizations and natural persons to be placed shares with and with a shareholding ratio lower than 5% were examined and approved at the 39th Meeting of the 3rd Board of Directors and the corresponding share subscription payment of RMB 480,972,579 was transferred into the relevant account of the Company, which would all be used for supplementing core assets of the Company; Shareholder qualifications of organizations with a shareholding ratio higher than 5%, investors outside Zhejiang Province and organizations for which the additional share issuance was tailed are being approved by banking regulatory authorities and the corresponding share subscription payment of RMB 1,022,707,494 is currently received as advance, which will be used for supplementing core assets of the Company, increasing the capital sufficiency rate of the Company, enhancing the Company's capability to withstand risks and overall strength after relevant approval formalities have been finished.

##### (iii) Use of Non-raised Funds in Reporting Period

During the reporting period, the Company did not have any non-raised funds investment project.

## VI. Changes in accounting policies and estimates or significant accounting errors

In the reporting period, in terms of the entrustment business where the client provided funds and the Company only provided agent service for distribution, supervision, use and assistance in collection of those funds according to the borrower, use, term and interest rate of funds determined by the client and where the client shouldered risks, gains and losses and responsibilities of the entrustment transaction and the Company only received commission charges from the entrustment transaction, it was measured off the balance sheet, which had been originally presented in the balance sheet. And the prospective application method was adopted in the said change of the accounting policy.

In the reporting period, no change occurred in accounting estimates. Nor there existed any correction of significant accounting errors.

## VII. Explanation on main accounting policies chosen and important accounting estimates

Based on the going-concern assumption and according to actual transactions and events, the Company recognized and measured transactions and events in line with the Enterprise Accounting Principles — Basic Principles and other accounting principles (hereinafter “the New Principles” or “the New Accounting Principles”) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance in

Feb. 2006. Financial statements prepared based on such recognition and measurement factually and completely presented the financial status, operating results, cash flows and other relevant information of the Company.

The Company adopted the accrual basis accounting system to recognize, measure and report transactions. Except for the fair value, the net realizable and the capitalized value mentioned in the Notes, other items were recorded at their historical cost. And measurement attributes of the reported items in the reporting period remained unchanged.

Measurement of transactions in foreign currencies: Transactions in different foreign currencies were recorded separately. The principle of preparing financial statements in RMB was to prepare statements in different currencies at first, then convert the currencies to USD and then convert USD to the amount in RMB so as to prepare the financial statements in RMB. On the balance sheet date, foreign-currency monetary items were converted into the recording currency according to the exchange rates on the balance sheet date, and the exchange difference caused thereupon was recorded into current gains and losses. Non-monetary foreign-currency items measured at their historical cost were converted according to the exchange rates on the transaction beginning date; Non-monetary foreign-currency items measured at their fair value were converted into RMB according to the exchange rates on the fair value recognition date, and difference between the amount in RMB and the amount in the original recording currency belonged to the foreign-currency non-monetary item of available-for-sale financial assets and were recorded into capital reserve. And difference of other items was recorded into current gains and losses.

## VIII. Material Asset Loss Caused by Joint Liability of the Company due to Guarantees Provided for External Parties

During the reporting period, the Company did not have to pay for any joint liability due to guarantees provided for external parties.

## IX. Liabilities of the Company, Changes in Counseling and Cash Arrangement for Repaying Convertible Bonds in Future

As at the period-end, balance of subordinated bonds issued by the Company stood at RMB 550 million, with a borrowing of RMB 300 million from the central bank. And the Company is quite capable to repay the said subordinated bonds and the principal and interest to the central bank.

## X. Major Changes in Operation Environment and Macroeconomic Policies and Regulations, as well as Their Influence

#### (i) Macro-control

In 2009, China adopted a moderately loose monetary policy to boost economic growth. However, the monetary policy actually operated was “too loose”. With a series of loose policies, domestic credit soared dramatically with high market liquidity and sharp increase of fixed asset investment. The real economy began to recover gradually.

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_1085_190_1150_358.jpg" alt="Image" width="5%" /></div>


In the face of the stricter macro-control, the Company actively enhanced its management over assets and liabilities and improved the asset structure. Firstly, it properly controlled loan issuance so as to maintain a steady and balanced loan growth. Secondly, it further adjusted the asset structure, increased and restricted loan issuance properly, optimized the loan structure, increased types of consumer loans, and expanded the consumer loan business. Thirdly, it properly adjusted ratios of assets with high liquidity such as investment, financing and notes, actively took part in operation in the note market, the money market and the bond market, and tried to increase profitability while maintaining liquidity. Finally, the Company made efforts to secure intermediary agent business and wealth management business to look for new profit generators. To sum up, the macro-control accelerated the Company's adjustment to its asset structure and income structure, with no material negative influence on the Company.

##### (ii) Changes in exchange rates

In 2009, the People's Bank of China continued deepening the reform in the foreign exchange management system. The RMB exchange rate formation mechanism was gradually optimized and the basic role of market demand and supply became increasingly important. With the international financial crisis and the affected domestic economy, the exchange rate of RMB against USD generally maintained stable between 6.82:1 and 6.84:1, with the RMB annual appreciation rate standing at only 0.12% for the year. Considering tendency of the RMB exchange rate over the year, the exchange rate policy of the central bank was more flexible and independent, i.e. to improve the RMB exchange rate formation mechanism according to principles of activity, controllability and progressiveness, as well as international capital flows and tendency changes of main currencies.

To rise to risks caused by exchange rate changes, the Company enhanced its operation risk consciousness and its ability to handle risks. Meanwhile, the Company enhanced its exchange rate risk prevention consciousness, improved management over exchange rate risk exposure, enhanced its ability to identify, measure and monitor exchange rate risks and optimized the internal risk control mechanism. It also adjusted its asset and liability structure in a timely and rational way according to exchange rate fluctuations.

##### (v) Changes in housing loan policy

In 2009, the State Council and the CBRC unveiled the Notice on Adjusting Capital Ratios of Fixed Asset Investment Projects (GF [2009] No.27), the Interim Management Measures for Fixed Assets Loans (CBRC 2009 Order No.2), and the Guidelines for Project Financing Business (YJF [2009] No.71). According to the said policies and in order to reinforce and improve property loan management, control real estate credit risks and promote sustained and healthy development of the property loan business of the Company, the Company formulated relevant management methods in a timely manner so as to enhance management over equity funds and credit funds for real estate projects and provide guidance for the real estate loan business. Meanwhile, the Company effectively strengthened examinations and checks on the real estate loan business, strictly controlled real estate loan risks and maintained high asset quality while promoting healthy development of the real estate loan business.

## XI. Problems and difficulties in operation, as well as relevant solutions

##### (i) Problems and difficulties in operation

During the reporting period, the Company encountered the following problems and difficulties in operation: Firstly, the business scale was restricted by limited capital. Secondly, affected by the macro-economic environment, non-performing loans were on the rise with great pressure on the Company to control increase of such loans. And the Company needed to improve its ability to control risks.

Thirdly, benchmark interest rates for deposits and loans went down and market liquidity was high, which led to decrease of return on assets and an obvious narrowing of net interest rate differential between assets and liabilities. Fourthly, human resource management was relatively weak, the personnel structural problem became more obvious, and the Company lacked high-rank managerial staff and professionals, all of which restricted constant improvement of the management capability and sound development of various businesses. Finally, the Company faced an external fraud risk due to more uncertainties in economic activities.

In order to solve problems mentioned above, the Company worked hard in aspects as follows:

1. The Company improved its marketing system and the mechanism to stabilize and increase deposits. As a result, the deposit scale maintained a stable growth. To be specific, in order to ensure a stable growth of basic deposits, the Company further improved its marketing mechanism by forging ahead month by month with the annual deposit increase plan, convening special meetings on a quarterly basis to analyze deposits, improving VIP entrance management in all banking branches, focusing on expanding basic customers, increasing the capital retention rate and other measures.

2. The Company paid close attention to changes in the economic and financial situation so as to formulate more prudent policy guidelines for the loan business for ensuring safety of assets and liquidity. Meanwhile, it optimized the asset and liability structure and improved quality and sustainability of its business performance so as to ensure sustained, steady and healthy development of all businesses. It also set up specialized teams in the headquarters, branches and sub-branches to provide service for third-grade small enterprises, optimized the credit operation procedure and increased efficiency in approving loans for small enterprises. The Company issued more loans to quality small enterprises and made three lists of the scale, management and examination of the small enterprises to which loans were issued.

3. The Company reasonably controlled the credit scale. It implemented plans to reduce and withdraw risky loans. At the same time, it adjusted the customer structure by maintaining cooperation with quality customers and terminating cooperation with low-quality ones so as to maximize credit benefit. The Company also adopted the credit risk executive officer mechanism, tried out the cooperation suspension and resumption mechanism for the credit business, and accomplished development and operation of the twelve-grade off-balance sheet business and the five-grade individual business with increasing accuracy in classifying credit assets.

4. The Company enhanced product innovation, cooperated with other parties in product development and constantly expanded the market share of its agent service business. It was successfully qualified for selling open-ended securities investment funds on a commission basis. Meanwhile, it increased its "software" and "hardware" input by launching new VIP cards and adding privileged services for settlement, wealth management, etc.. It developed a wealth management product selling system covering 25 functions, shifting sale of wealth management products from hand operation to electronic operation.

5. The Company improved its internal management and increased compliance with regulations. To be specific, it strengthened investigation and punishment on behaviors in violation of regulations and rules, making great efforts in preventing regulation violation and moral risks. It also enhanced basic accounting management and increased quality and efficiency of counter service. Meanwhile, the Company stuck to the principle of "attaching great importance to talents so as to boost development of the bank" and carried out a talent strategy of "introducing great talents, promoting great development, formulating a terraced management system and stabilizing the talent team".

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_1086_190_1150_358.jpg" alt="Image" width="5%" /></div>


6. The Company carried out a capital supplementation plan by initiating capital increase in a timely way, and conducting a share placement and targeted additional share issuance, which made sure that the capital sufficiency rate of the Company met relevant requirements. And the Company optimized its branch layout by setting up the Beibaixiang sub-branch within the year, relocating three sub-branches, and opening up the Quzhou Jiangshan sub-branch and the Hangzhou branch. Meanwhile, the Company was approved by CBRC to set up the Shanghai branch, representing a significant breakthrough in the cross-regional operation.

#### (ii) Outlook of future development and measures to be taken

## 1. Influence of development trends in the banking sector and market competition on the Company

Cross-administrative region operation of financial institutions in the Yangtze River Delta has become more and more frequent with increasing cross-administrative region capital flows. Zhejiang Province has been making great efforts in developing the modern financial industry, which has attracted funds and talents at home and abroad. Meanwhile, fast development of banking institutions in Wenzhou, great government support for development of small and medium enterprises, and healthy interaction between the local economy and the financial market all provide a wide space for development of city commercial banks.

## 2. Development opportunities and challenges in the future with the management's attention

### (1) Opportunities

China enjoys a thriving market with the continued progress of industrialization and urbanization and the unchanged long-term economic growing trend and advantage. In addition, a series of effective measures have been adopted by the government to boost economic growth and the macro-control policies begin to function, which are expected to maintain a stable and fast development of the national economy. Commercial banks and other financial institutions will continue carrying forward various reforms, improving corporate governance, strengthening basic management, internal control and risk prevention mechanisms, and correctly handling the relationship between the economy boosting role of the financial industry and prevention of financial risks so as to make the financial industry function better in boosting economy.

##### (2) Challenges

Wenzhou is one of those cities with the richest variety and the largest number of banking institutions in China. More and more financial institutions come to Wenzhou for business, which enriches the variety of local financial institutions, promotes service improvement in those institutions and yet results in a fiercer market competition among those financial institutions, as well as a fiercer competition for financial and talent resources. As such, the regulatory authority will enhance risk control in the banking industry. Besides requirements set for the capital sufficiency rate, prudent indexes will also be adopted in terms of asset quality, provision coverage, deposit-loan ratios, liquidity, etc..

## 3. Risk factors that may impact the Company's future development strategies and operation objectives

Due to the increasing uncertainty in macroeconomic development, the system risk and credit risk of the banking sector should not be neglected. The increasingly reinforced capital regulation puts more pressure on small and mid-sized commercial banks' supplementing capital. Under the circumstances that the traditional business more has no substantial change and financial disintermediation, the profit ability of the Company tends to be affected more by the increasing credit cost and decreasing interest rate differential. The monetary policy is adjusted frequently and the interest rate market reform is accelerated, bringing new challenges to the Company's ability to resist economic fluctuation and control financial risks.

#### (i) Guidelines for business development in 2010

With both opportunities and challenges brought by social and economic development, the Company will implement the Scientific Development Concept, as well as monetary and credit policies. With the objective to build a “quality retail bank”, the Company will enhance internal control risk management, standardize internal operation, improve the strategic development planning, strengthen reform and innovation, and promote transformation and upgrading so as to achieve fast and healthy development in all businesses.

### (ii) Business goals for 2010

Various deposits increase by RMB 9 billion over the year and the balance at the end of the year reaches RMB 42.7 billion. Various loans increase by RMB 4.139 billion and the balance at the end of the year reaches RMB 28.484 billion. Total assets increase by RMB 8.8 billion and the balance at the end of the year reaches RMB 50.3 billion. At the end of the year, the asset sufficiency rate is above 12% and the net profit reaches RMB 501 million.

#### (iii) Main measures to be taken for business development

Firstly, to accelerate management transformation, improve the management system and basically set up the headquarters, branch and sub-branch three-level management structure. Secondly, to speed up business transformation, clarify target customers and increase market competitiveness. Thirdly, to accelerate structural transformation, reduce administrative cost and increase the sustainable development ability. Fourthly, to improve corporate governance capability and perfect the capital supplementation mechanism so as to provide institutional and mechanism guarantee for perpetual operation. Fifthly, to improve internal management, enhance risk control and realize effective and intensive operation. Sixthly, to improve overall staff quality, stick to the principle of "attaching great importance to talents so as to boost development of the bank", and build a high-quality and compound talent terrace. Seventhly, to increase core competitiveness, give play to the brand effect and demonstrate the uniqueness of the Company as a quality retail bank. Eighthly, to improve service and deepen the cultural and ethical improvement campaign so as to create a good social image. Ninthly, to give play to the leading and supporting role of technology and further promote informatization. And finally, to improve the leading team and the ability to take a leading position in scientific development.

## XIII. Routine work of the Board of Directors

### (i) Meetings of the Board and Resolutions

During the reporting period, the Company held 12 meetings of the Board in total, including 4 routine meetings and 8 interim meetings, and totally discussed 73 proposals. Of which, fifty-four major resolutions such as establishing Hangzhou Branch and Shanghai Branch, offering hybrid capital bonds, implementing capital and share increase and purchasing business occupancy for Jiangshan Sub-Branch were decided at the meetings, bringing into play its core role in real earnest.

1. On 16 Jan. 2009, the Company held the 29 $ ^{th} $ meeting of the 3 $ ^{rd} $ Board of Directors at the Victoria Grand Hotel Wenzhou, 18 directors should vote at the meeting, and 16 of them actually voted at the meeting. Seventeen important issues were discussed and passed at this meeting, i.e., the Report on Business Plan Fulfillment in 2008 and Business Plan for 2009 (Draft), Financial Final Report for 2008 and Financial Budget Report for 2009 (Draft), Profit Distribution Preplan for 2008 (Draft), the Report on

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_1086_190_1150_358.jpg" alt="Image" width="5%" /></div>


Related Transaction between the Bank and its Related Parties in 2008 (Draft), the Proposal on Amending Detailed Rules for Implementation on Related Transaction Management, the Proposal on Provision for Line of Employee's Consumption Loan and Relevant Interests, the Proposal on Measures for Administration of Performance Appraisal for the Top Management in 2009, the Proposal on Further Raising Employees' Income, the Proposal on Development Plan for 2009, the Proposal on Establishing Hangzhou Branch, the Proposal on Planning to Issue Hybrid Capital Bonds, the Report on Implementation of Rectification to Regulatory Opinion (Draft), the Authorization Suggestion for 2009; Report on Adjusting Development Plan for 2008-2010 of Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd., the Working Plan of the Board of Directors for 2009, the Proposal on Equity Transfer of Wenzhou Electric Power Construction Co., Ltd., the Proposal on Obtaining Property Rights of Building Located at the South End of 8/F of Head Office by Means of Replacement. The meeting also deliberate on the Report on Risk Analysis and Appraisal, the 4th Quarterly Report for 2008, the Report on the Management's Business Goal Fulfillment in 2008 and the Rewarding Method for Over-fulfilling Profit, the Report on Assessment of Special Treatment of Case in the Past Three Years and Work Schedule for 2009, the Special Audit Report on Related Transaction between Insiders and Shareholders, the Report of Resolutions Executed by the Board of 2008, the Three-Year Information Development Plan for 2008-2010 of Bank of Wenzhou and the Thematic Report on President Work Meeting for 2009.

2. On 2 Apr. 2009, the Company held the 30 $ ^{th} $ meeting of the 3 $ ^{rd} $ Board of Directors by voting through correspondence. 18 directors should vote at the meeting, and actually all of them voted at the meeting. The Agenda for the Shareholders' General Meeting 2008 of Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd. and the Annual Report 2008 of Bank of Wenzhou were discussed and passed at this meeting.

3. On 3 Apr. 2009, the Company held the 31 $ ^{st} $ meeting of the 3 $ ^{rd} $ Board of Directors by voting through correspondence. 18 directors should vote at the meeting, and actually all of them voted at the meeting. The following proposals were discussed and passed at this meeting, i.e., the Proposal on Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd. Intending to Offer Hybrid Capital Bonds and Offer Plan, the Proposal on Authorizing the Board and the Management to Handle Specific Matters on Issuing Hybrid Capital Bond by Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd. and Proposal on Utilization of Raised Capital through Issuing Hybrid Capital Bond by Bank of Wenzhou Co., Ltd. and Feasibility Analysis.
